S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\uplus S_{{\mathrm{2}}}=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.
Let
S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
be a type substitution such that

\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})=\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{2}}})\setminus(\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{2}}})\setminus\{\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\})
and

S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{Y})=S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{Y})
for any
\mathit{Y}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})
.
Note that
S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}})
and
S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}})
.
Thus
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}=[\mathit{X}:=S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\!\rightarrow\!S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}})]\uplus S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.
Since
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\uplus S_{{\mathrm{2}}}=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}=[\mathit{X}:=S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\!\rightarrow\!S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}})]\uplus S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
,
there exist some
S^{\prime\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
such that

•:

S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}=S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\uplus S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\uplus S^{\prime\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and

•:

S_{{\mathrm{2}}}=[\mathit{X}:=S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\!\rightarrow\!S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}})]\uplus S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\uplus S^{\prime\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

Here,

\begin{array}[]{lll}S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f})&=&S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E})[S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(w^{\prime}):\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X}]\\
&\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}&S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E}[S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(w^{\prime}):\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}]))\\
&=&S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}]))\\
&=&S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}]))\\
&&\text{(since $S_{{\mathrm{1}}}$ is generated by DTI and $\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})\cap\{\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\}=\emptyset$)}\\
&=&S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\end{array}

We have
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])=\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\uplus S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\uplus S^{\prime\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,\mathit{r}
.
Here,

\begin{array}[]{lll}\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}})&=&\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}]))\\
&=&\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E}[w^{\prime}]))\cup\{\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\}\\
&=&(\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{E}[w^{\prime}])\setminus\{\mathit{X}\})\cup\{\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\}\\
&=&(\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}}])\setminus\{\mathit{X}\})\cup\{\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\}\\
&\supseteq&(\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})\setminus\{\mathit{X}\})\cup\{\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\}\\
&\supseteq&\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})\qquad(\text{since $\mathit{X}\,\not\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})$})\end{array}

By the IH, there exist some
S^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
such that

S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,\mathit{r}
and

\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\uplus S^{\prime\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})
and,
for any
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})
,
\textit{ftv}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}}))\cap\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})=\emptyset
.

Let
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})
.
Since
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\neq\mathit{X}
and
\textit{ftv}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}}))\cap\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})=\emptyset
by the IH,
we finish.

Otherwise:
:

We are given
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}=[]
and
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}=S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\uplus S_{{\mathrm{2}}}
. We have

S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f^{\prime}})\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})
; note that type variables generated by term
substitution is not captured by
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}
since
\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})\subseteq\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{f})
.
We finish by the IH.
∎

Lemma A.22.

If
\mathit{f}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,\textsf{{blame}}\,\ell
, then, for any
S^{\prime}
such that type variables in

\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime})
are disjoint from ones generated during the evaluation, there
exist
S^{\prime\prime}
and
\ell^{\prime}
such that
S^{\prime}(\mathit{f})\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime\prime}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,\textsf{{blame}}\,\ell^{\prime}
.

Proof.

By induction on the length of the evaluation sequence.

Case the length is 0:
:

Obvious.

Case the length is more than 0:
:

We are given
\mathit{f}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,\mathit{f^{\prime}}
and

\mathit{f^{\prime}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,\textsf{{blame}}\,\ell
for some
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}
,
S_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, and
\mathit{f^{\prime}}

such that
S=S_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.
By case analysis on the evaluation rule applied to
\mathit{f}
.

Case (
E_Step
):
:

We are given
\mathit{f}=\mathit{E}[\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}]
and

\mathit{f^{\prime}}=S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])
and

\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}

for some
\mathit{E}
,
\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
, and
\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
.
By case analysis on the reduction rule applied to
\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
.

Case (
R_InstBase
):
:

We are given
w:\iota\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X}\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[\mathit{X}:=\iota]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,w

for some
w
,
\iota
,
\mathit{X}
,
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}
, and
\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.
By case analysis on
S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})
.

Case
S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})=\iota
:
:

S^{\prime}(w:\iota\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X})\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S^{\prime}(w)

by (
R_IdBase
).
Thus,
S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])
.
Since
{[S1={X:->iota}]}
and
S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})=\iota
,
we have
S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])=S^{\prime}(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}]))
.
Thus,
S^{\prime}(\mathit{f})\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S^{\prime}(\mathit{f^{\prime}})
.
By the IH, we finish.

Case
S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})=\iota^{\prime}
for some
\iota^{\prime}\neq\iota
:
:

We have
S^{\prime}(w:\iota\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X})\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,\textsf{{blame}}\,\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.
Thus,
S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\textsf{{blame}}\,\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}])\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\textsf{{blame}}\,\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

Case
S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})=\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
:
:

We have
S^{\prime}(w:\iota\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X})\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S^{\prime}(w):\iota\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
Thus,
S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\textsf{{blame}}\,\ell:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}])\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\textsf{{blame}}\,\ell
.

Case
S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})=\mathit{X}
:
:

We have
S^{\prime}(w:\iota\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X})\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S^{\prime}(w)
.
Thus,
S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[w])
.
Since
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[w])=S^{\prime}\circ S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E}[w])
, we finish by the IH.

Case
S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
for some
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\neq\mathit{X}
:
:

We have
S^{\prime}(w:\iota\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X})\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[\mathit{X^{\prime}}:=\iota]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S^{\prime}(w)
.
By (
E_Step
),

S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[\mathit{X^{\prime}}:=\iota]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,[\mathit{X^{\prime}}:=\iota]\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[w])
.
Let
S^{\prime\prime}
be a type substitution such that

S^{\prime}=S^{\prime\prime}\uplus[\mathit{X}:=\mathit{X^{\prime}}]
.
Since
[\mathit{X^{\prime}}:=\iota]\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[w])=([\mathit{X^{\prime}}:=\iota]\circ S^{\prime\prime})\uplus[\mathit{X}:=\iota](\mathit{E}[w])=[\mathit{X^{\prime}}:=\iota]\circ S^{\prime\prime}(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E}[w]))
.
By the IH, we finish.

Case (
R_InstArrow
):
:

We are given
w:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X}\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[\mathit{X}:=\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,w:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}

for some
w
,
\mathit{X}
,
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}
,
\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, and fresh
\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
By case analysis on
S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})
.

Case
S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})=\iota
:
:

Since
{[S^{\prime}(v:*->*=>l1*=>l2X)-->A{}blamel2]}
,
we finish.

Case
S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})=\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
:
:

We have
S^{\prime}(w:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X})\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S^{\prime}(w):\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
Thus,
S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[S^{\prime}(w):\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}])
.
By the assumption,
\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\,\not\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime})
.
Since we can suppose that
\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\,\not\in\,\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{E}[w])
,
we have
S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[S^{\prime}(w):\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}])=S^{\prime}\uplus[\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:=\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:=\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}](\mathit{E}[w:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}])
.
By the IH, we finish.

Case
S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})=\mathit{X}
:
:

We have
S^{\prime}(w:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X})\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[\mathit{X}:=\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S^{\prime}(w):\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
Thus,
S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])
.
Let
S^{\prime\prime}
be a type substitution such that

\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime})=\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime})
and

S^{\prime\prime}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(S^{\prime}(\mathit{X^{\prime}}))
for any
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime})
.
Since
\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\,\not\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime})
,
we have
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])=S^{\prime\prime}(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}]))
.
By the IH, we finish.

Case
S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
for some
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\neq\mathit{X}
:
:

We have
S^{\prime}(w:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X})\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[\mathit{X^{\prime}}:=\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S^{\prime}(w):\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
Thus,
S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[\mathit{X^{\prime}}:=\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,[\mathit{X^{\prime}}:=\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}]\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])
.
Let
S^{\prime\prime}
be a type substitution such that

S^{\prime}=[\mathit{X}:=\mathit{X^{\prime}}]\uplus S^{\prime\prime}
.
Then, since
\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\,\not\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime})
, we have

[\mathit{X^{\prime}}:=\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}]\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])=([\mathit{X^{\prime}}:=\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}]\circ S^{\prime\prime})\uplus[\mathit{X}:=\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}](\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}])([\mathit{X^{\prime}}:=\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}]\circ S^{\prime\prime})(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}]))
.
Thus, by the IH, we finish.

Case (
R_LetP
):
:

Since
S^{\prime}
does not capture type variables generated by the value substitution,
we finish by the IH.

Otherwise:
:

Obvious by the IH.

Case (
E_Blame
):
:

Obvious.
∎

\thmSoundness

*

Proof.

(1)

We prove below an equivalent statement that, if

\mathit{f}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\uplus S_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,\mathit{r}
where
\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})\subseteq\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{f})
and

\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{2}}})\cap\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{f})=\emptyset
, then, for any
S^{\prime}
such that

\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f})))=\emptyset
,
S^{\prime}(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f}))\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,S^{\prime}(\mathit{r})
for
some
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
. (From the conditions on
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
S_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, it is
clear that
S^{\prime}(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f}))=S^{\prime}(S(\mathit{f}))
where
S=S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\uplus S_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.)

By Lemma
2
,

S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f})\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,\mathit{r}
for some
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}

such that
\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{2}}})
and,
for any
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})
,
\textit{ftv}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X}))\cap\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})=\emptyset
.
Let
S^{\prime\prime}
be a type substitution such that

\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime})=\textit{ftv}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f}))
and

S^{\prime\prime}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})
for any
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime})
.
Since
\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{2}}})\cap\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{f})=\emptyset
, we have

\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})\cap\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{f})=\emptyset
.
Furthermore,
for any
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})
,
\textit{ftv}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X}))\cap\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})=\emptyset
,
and
\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime})=\textit{ftv}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f}))
.
Thus,
\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime})\cap\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})=\emptyset
.
Without loss of generality, we can suppose that

\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime})
are disjoint from type variables generated by

S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f})\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,\mathit{r}
.
Since types to which
S^{\prime\prime}
maps have no free type variables,
we have
S^{\prime\prime}(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f}))\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,S^{\prime\prime}(\mathit{r})

by Lemma
A.21
.
Since
S^{\prime\prime}(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f}))
has no free type variables,
free type variables in
S^{\prime\prime}(\mathit{r})
are generated during the
evaluation; thus, we can suppose that
S^{\prime\prime}(\mathit{r})=S^{\prime}(\mathit{r})
.
Since
S^{\prime\prime}(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f}))=S^{\prime}(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f}))
,
we have
S^{\prime}(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{f}))\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,S^{\prime}(\mathit{r})
.

(2)

We can suppose that type variables generated by the evaluation

\mathit{f}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,\textsf{{blame}}\,\ell
are disjoint from
\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime})
.
Thus, we finish by Lemma
A.22
.

(3)

Suppose
S(\mathit{f})\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,w^{\prime}
for some
S^{\prime}
and
w^{\prime}
.
Then, by Theorem
5
, it must be the case that

\mathit{f}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime\prime}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,w
for some
w
and
S^{\prime\prime}
, contradicting
\mathit{f}\!\Uparrow
.
∎

A.6.
Correctness of Cast Insertion and Type Safety of the ITGL

Definition A.5.

\Gamma\rightsquigarrow\Gamma^{\prime}
is the least relation satisfying the following rules.

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\end{array}}{\emptyset\rightsquigarrow\emptyset}\ \textsc{{{CI\_Empty}}{}}}

{\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{c}\Gamma\rightsquigarrow\Gamma^{\prime}\quad\overrightarrow{\mathit{Y_{\mathit{j}}}}\cap\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{U})=\emptyset\\
\end{array}}{\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}.\mathit{U}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma^{\prime},\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{Y_{\mathit{j}}}}.\mathit{U}}\ \textsc{{{CI\_ExtendVar}}{}}}

Lemma A.23.

If
\Gamma\vdash v\rightsquigarrow\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}
, then
\mathit{f}=w
for some
w
.

Proof.

Straightforward by case analysis on the cast insertion rule applied last to
derive
\Gamma\vdash v\rightsquigarrow\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}
.
∎

Lemma A.24.

If
\Gamma\vdash e:\mathit{U}
and
\Gamma\rightsquigarrow\Gamma^{\prime}
,
then
\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash e\rightsquigarrow\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}
for some
\mathit{f}
.

Proof.

By induction on the derivation of
\Gamma\vdash e:\mathit{U}
.

Case (
IT_VarP
):
:

We are given
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{x}:\mathit{U^{\prime}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]

for some
\mathit{x}
,
\mathit{U^{\prime}}
,
\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}
, and
\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}
and, by inversion,

\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}.\mathit{U^{\prime}}\,\in\,\Gamma
.
Since
\Gamma\rightsquigarrow\Gamma^{\prime}
, we have

\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{Y_{\mathit{j}}}}.\mathit{U^{\prime}}\,\in\,\Gamma^{\prime}
for some
\overrightarrow{\mathit{Y_{\mathit{j}}}}
such that

\overrightarrow{\mathit{Y_{\mathit{j}}}}\cap\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{U^{\prime}})=\emptyset
.
Let
\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{k}}}}
be the concatenation of
\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}
and
\overrightarrow{\mathit{Y_{\mathit{j}}}}
and

\overrightarrow{\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}_{\mathit{k}}}
be a sequence of optional types such that:
if
\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{n}}}\,\in\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{k}}}}
appears in
\mathit{U^{\prime}}
(thus
\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{n}}}\,\in\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}
),

\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}_{\mathit{n}}=\mathit{T_{\mathit{j}}}
where
\mathit{j}
is a number such that
\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{n}}}=\mathit{X_{\mathit{j}}}\,\in\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}
; and
if
\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{n}}}\,\in\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{k}}}}
does not appear in
\mathit{U^{\prime}}
,
\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}^{\prime}_{\mathit{n}}=\nu
.
Then,
\mathit{U^{\prime}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]=\mathit{U^{\prime}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{k}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}_{\mathit{k}}}]
.
By (
CI_VarP
), we have

\Gamma\vdash\mathit{x}\rightsquigarrow\mathit{x}[\overrightarrow{\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}_{\mathit{i}}}]:\mathit{U^{\prime}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]
.

Case (
IT_Const
):
:

By (
CI_Const
).

Case (
IT_Op
):
:

We are given
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{op}\,(e_{{\mathrm{1}}},e_{{\mathrm{2}}}):\iota
for some
\mathit{op}
,
e_{{\mathrm{1}}}
,

e_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, and
\iota
and, by inversion,

•:

\Gamma\vdash e_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
,

•:

\Gamma\vdash e_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
,

•:

\mathit{ty}(\mathit{op})=\iota_{{\mathrm{1}}}\!\rightarrow\!\iota_{{\mathrm{2}}}\!\rightarrow\!\iota
, and

•:

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\iota_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sim\iota_{{\mathrm{2}}}

for some
\iota_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\iota_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.
By the IHs,
\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash e_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rightsquigarrow\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash e_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rightsquigarrow\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}

for some
\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
By (
CI_Op
), we have

\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash\mathit{op}\,(e_{{\mathrm{1}}},e_{{\mathrm{2}}})\rightsquigarrow\mathit{op}\,(\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\iota_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\!\iota_{{\mathrm{2}}}):\iota

for some
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

Case (
IT_AbsI
):
:

By the IH and (
CI_AbsI
)
since
\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\mathit{T}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma^{\prime},\mathit{x}:\mathit{T}
.

Case (
IT_AbsE
):
:

By the IH and (
CI_AbsE
)
since
\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\mathit{U}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma^{\prime},\mathit{x}:\mathit{U}
.

Case (
IT_App
):
:

By the IHs and (
CI_App
).

Case (
IT_LetP
):
:

We are given

\Gamma\vdash\textsf{{let}}\,\mathit{x}=v_{{\mathrm{1}}}\textsf{{ in }}e_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U}
for some
\mathit{x}
,
v_{{\mathrm{1}}}
, and
e_{{\mathrm{2}}}

and, by inversion,

•:

\Gamma\vdash v_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
,

•:

\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}.\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash e_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U}
, and

•:

\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}=\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\setminus(\textit{ftv}\,(\Gamma)\cup\textit{ftv}\,(v_{{\mathrm{1}}}))

for some
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
.
By the IH and Lemma
A.23
,
\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash v_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rightsquigarrow w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}

for some
w_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.
Since
\textit{ftv}\,(\Gamma^{\prime})=\textit{ftv}\,(\Gamma)
,

\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}=\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\setminus(\textit{ftv}\,(\Gamma^{\prime})\cup\textit{ftv}\,(v_{{\mathrm{1}}}))
.
Let
\overrightarrow{\mathit{Y_{\mathit{j}}}}=\textit{ftv}\,(w_{{\mathrm{1}}})\setminus(\textit{ftv}\,(\Gamma^{\prime})\cup\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\cup\textit{ftv}\,(v_{{\mathrm{1}}}))
.
Since
\Gamma\rightsquigarrow\Gamma^{\prime}
, we have

\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}.\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma^{\prime},\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{Y_{\mathit{i}}}}.\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
.
Thus, by the IH,

\Gamma^{\prime},\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{Y_{\mathit{i}}}}.\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash e_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rightsquigarrow\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{U}
for some
\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
By (
CI_LetP
),

\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash\textsf{{let}}\,\mathit{x}=v_{{\mathrm{1}}}\textsf{{ in }}e_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rightsquigarrow\textsf{{let}}\,\mathit{x}=\Lambda\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}\overrightarrow{\mathit{Y_{\mathit{j}}}}.\,w_{{\mathrm{1}}}\textsf{{ in }}\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{U}
.
∎

Lemma A.25.

If
\Gamma\vdash e\rightsquigarrow\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}
, then
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}
.

Proof.

By induction on the derivation of
\Gamma\vdash e\rightsquigarrow\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}
.

Case (
CI_VarP
):
:

By (
T_VarP
).

Case (
CI_Const
):
:

By (
T_Const
).

Case (
CI_Op
):
:

By the IHs, (
T_Cast
), and (
T_Op
).

Case (
CI_AbsI
):
:

By the IH and (
T_Abs
).

Case (
CI_AbsE
):
:

By the IH and (
T_Abs
).

Case (
CI_App
):
:

By the IHs, (
T_App
), and the fact that

\mathit{U}\triangleright\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
implies
\mathit{U}\sim\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

Case (
CI_LetP
):
:

By the IHs and (
T_LetP
). ∎

\thmCastInsertion

*

Proof.

Since
\Gamma\rightsquigarrow\Gamma
, we conclude by Lemmas
A.24
and

A.25
.
∎

\corTypeSafeITGL

*

Proof.

By Theorems
1
and
5
.
∎

A.7.
Gradual Guarantee

A.7.1.
Static Gradual Guarantee in ITGL

Lemma A.26.

If
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{[]}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{[]}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
,
then
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
. Base cases follow from simple case analysis
on
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{[]}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{[]}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
Case
C_Arrow
is easy.
∎

Lemma A.27.

If
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
and
\mathit{U^{\prime}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}}
, then

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}}
.

Proof.

By induction on the sum of the sizes of derivations of
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
and
\mathit{U^{\prime}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}}
.
∎

Lemma A.28.

If
\langle\Gamma\vdash e:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:e^{\prime}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
and
\langle\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash e^{\prime}:\mathit{U^{\prime}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}}:e^{\prime\prime}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime\prime}\rangle
, then

\langle\Gamma\vdash e:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}})}\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}}:e^{\prime\prime}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime\prime}\rangle
.

Proof.

By induction on
e^{\prime}
. Use Lemma
A.27
.

∎

Lemma A.29.

If
\langle\Gamma\vdash e:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}}):S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(e^{\prime})\dashv S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\Gamma^{\prime})\rangle
, then
\langle\Gamma\vdash e:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:e^{\prime}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
.

Proof.

By induction on
\langle\Gamma\vdash e:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}}):S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(e^{\prime})\dashv S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\Gamma^{\prime})\rangle
.

∎

Lemma A.30.

If

\Gamma\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\Gamma^{\prime}
and

e\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}e^{\prime}
and

S(\Gamma)\vdash S(e):\mathit{U}
, then
there exist
S^{\prime}
and
\mathit{U^{\prime}}
such that

S^{\prime}(\Gamma^{\prime})\vdash S^{\prime}(e^{\prime}):\mathit{U^{\prime}}
and

\langle S(\Gamma)\vdash S(e):\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{[]}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:S^{\prime}(e^{\prime})\dashv S^{\prime}(\Gamma^{\prime})\rangle
.

Proof.

We show that there exists
\mathit{U^{\prime}}
such that

(S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})(\Gamma^{\prime})\vdash S^{\prime}(e^{\prime}):\mathit{U^{\prime}}
and

\langle S(\Gamma)\vdash S(e):\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{[]}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:(S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})(e^{\prime})\dashv(S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})(\Gamma^{\prime})\rangle

by induction on
e\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}e^{\prime}
.

Most cases are straightforward; Case
IP_App
follows from Lemma
A.26
.

∎

Lemma A.31.

If

S^{\prime}(\Gamma)\vdash S^{\prime}(e):\mathit{U^{\prime}}
, then
there exist some
S
and
S^{\prime\prime}
and
\mathit{U}
such that

PT(\Gamma,e)=(S,\mathit{U})
and

S(\Gamma)\vdash S(e):\mathit{U}
and

\langle S^{\prime}(\Gamma)\vdash S^{\prime}(e):\mathit{U^{\prime}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}}\mathit{U}:S(e)\dashv S(\Gamma)\rangle
.

Proof.

By completeness of
PT
, there exists
S
,
S^{\prime\prime}
and
U
such that

S(\Gamma)\vdash S(e):\mathit{U}
and
S^{\prime}=S^{\prime\prime}\circ S
and
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=S^{\prime\prime}(\mathit{U})
.
Since precision is reflexive, we have

\langle S^{\prime}(\Gamma)\vdash S^{\prime}(e):\mathit{U^{\prime}}\sqsubseteq_{[]}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:S^{\prime}(e)\dashv S^{\prime}(\Gamma)\rangle
.
Lemma
A.29
finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma A.32.

If

\langle\Gamma\vdash e:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:e^{\prime}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
and
\Gamma\rightsquigarrow\Gamma_{{\mathrm{0}}}
and
\Gamma^{\prime}\rightsquigarrow\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}
, then
there exist
\mathit{f}
and
\mathit{f^{\prime}}
such that

\Gamma_{{\mathrm{0}}}\vdash e\rightsquigarrow\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}
and

\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\vdash e^{\prime}\rightsquigarrow\mathit{f^{\prime}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}}
and

\langle\Gamma_{{\mathrm{0}}}\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\rangle
.

Proof.

By induction on
\langle\Gamma\vdash e:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:e^{\prime}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
.

∎

Lemma A.33.

If

\Gamma\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\Gamma^{\prime}
and

e\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}e^{\prime}
and

PT(\Gamma,e)=(S,\mathit{U})
, then
there exist
S^{\prime}
,
S^{\prime\prime}
,
\mathit{U^{\prime}}
,
\mathit{f}
and
\mathit{f^{\prime}}

such that

PT(\Gamma^{\prime},e^{\prime})=(S^{\prime},\mathit{U^{\prime}})
and

S(\Gamma)\vdash S(e)\rightsquigarrow\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}
and

S^{\prime}(\Gamma^{\prime})\vdash S^{\prime}(e^{\prime})\rightsquigarrow\mathit{f^{\prime}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}}
and

\langle S(\Gamma)\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv S^{\prime}(\Gamma^{\prime})\rangle
.

Proof.

By soundness of
PT
,
S(\Gamma)\vdash S(e):\mathit{U}
.
By Lemma
A.30
, there exist
S^{\prime}
,
\mathit{U^{\prime}}
such that

S^{\prime}(\Gamma^{\prime})\vdash S^{\prime}(e^{\prime}):\mathit{U^{\prime}}
and

\langle S(\Gamma)\vdash S(e):\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{[]}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:S^{\prime}(e^{\prime})\dashv S^{\prime}(\Gamma^{\prime})\rangle.

Since
S^{\prime}(\Gamma^{\prime})\vdash S^{\prime}(e^{\prime}):\mathit{U^{\prime}}
,
by Lemma
A.31
, there exist
S^{\prime\prime}
,
S^{\prime\prime\prime}
, and
\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}}

such that

PT(\Gamma^{\prime},e^{\prime})=(S^{\prime\prime},\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}})
and

S^{\prime\prime}(\Gamma^{\prime})\vdash S^{\prime\prime}(e^{\prime}):\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}}
and

\langle S^{\prime}(\Gamma^{\prime})\vdash S^{\prime}(e^{\prime}):\mathit{U^{\prime}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime\prime}}\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}}:S^{\prime\prime}(e^{\prime})\dashv S^{\prime\prime}(\Gamma^{\prime})\rangle.

By Lemma
A.28
,

\langle S(\Gamma)\vdash S(e):\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime\prime}}\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}}:S^{\prime\prime}(e^{\prime})\dashv S^{\prime\prime}(\Gamma^{\prime})\rangle
.
Finally, by Lemma
A.32
,
there exist
\mathit{f}
and
\mathit{f^{\prime}}
such that

S(\Gamma)\vdash S(e)\rightsquigarrow\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}
and

S^{\prime\prime}(\Gamma^{\prime})\vdash S^{\prime\prime}(e^{\prime})\rightsquigarrow\mathit{f^{\prime}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}}
and

\langle S(\Gamma)\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime\prime}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv S^{\prime\prime}(\Gamma^{\prime})\rangle
. ∎

\thmStaticGG

*

Proof.

Follows from Lemma
A.33
as a special case. (It is easy to see
that
\langle S(\Gamma)\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv S^{\prime}(\Gamma^{\prime})\rangle
implies

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.)
∎

A.7.2.
Dynamic Gradual Guarantee in
\lambda^{\textsf{DTI}}_{\textsf{B}}

Lemma A.34.

If
\langle\Gamma\vdash f:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
,
then
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

Proof.

By induction on the term precision derivation.
∎

Lemma A.35.

If
\langle\Gamma\vdash f:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
,
then
\Gamma\vdash f:\mathit{U}
and
\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash f^{\prime}:\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

Proof.

By induction on the term precision derivation.
∎

Lemma A.36.

If
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{X}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{X}
,
then
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}=\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

Proof.

By case analysis on the type precision rule applied last.
∎

Lemma A.37.

If
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{G_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{G_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
,
then
\mathit{G_{{\mathrm{1}}}}=\mathit{G_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

Proof.

If
\mathit{G_{{\mathrm{1}}}}=\iota
, then
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}=\iota
because the rules that can be applied
to derive
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S}\iota
are only (
P_IdBase
).
If
\mathit{G_{{\mathrm{2}}}}=\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
, then
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}=\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{21}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{22}}}}
for some
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{21}}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{22}}}}

because the rules that can be applied to derive
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star

are only (
P_Arrow
).
However,
\iota\sim\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{21}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{22}}}}
is contradictory.
We can also prove the case of
\mathit{G_{{\mathrm{1}}}}=\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
similarly.
∎

Lemma A.38.

If
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{G}
and
w:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
is a value,
then
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{G}
.

Proof.

Since
w:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
is a value,
we have two cases on
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
to be considered.
The case
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}=\star
leads to a contradiction because
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{G}
.
Otherwise,
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}=\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{21}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{22}}}}
for some
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{21}}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{22}}}}
.
Then,
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}=\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
for some
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{11}}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
, and

\mathit{G}=\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
.
Thus, (
P_Arrow
),
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{G}
.
∎

Lemma A.39.

If
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and

\langle\emptyset\vdash w:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\star:(w^{\prime}:\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star)\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,
then
\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}=\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

Proof.

By induction on the term precision derivation.
There are three interesting cases.

Case (
P_Cast
):
:

We are given

\langle\emptyset\vdash(w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}):\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\star:(w^{\prime}:\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star)\dashv\emptyset\rangle
and,
by inversion,

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:w^{\prime}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\mathit{U}
.
By Lemma
A.34
,
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
Since
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
, we have
\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}=\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}

by Lemma
A.37
.

Case (
P_CastL
):
:

We are given

\langle\emptyset\vdash(w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}):\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\star:(w^{\prime}:\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star)\dashv\emptyset\rangle
and,
by inversion,

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S}\star:(w^{\prime}:\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star)\dashv\emptyset\rangle
and

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\mathit{U}
.
Since
w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}
is a value,
we have
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
by Lemma
A.38
.
Thus, we finish by the IH.

Case (
P_CastR
):
:

We are given

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\star:(w^{\prime}:\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star)\dashv\emptyset\rangle
and,
by inversion,

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:w^{\prime}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.
By Lemma
A.34
,

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
Since
\mathit{U}\sim\mathit{U}
and
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
,
we have
\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}=\mathit{G^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
by Lemma
A.37
.
∎

Lemma A.40.

For any
S
.

(1)

If
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
,
then
S(\mathit{U})\sqsubseteq_{S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

(2)

If
\langle\Gamma\vdash f:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
,
then
\langle S(\Gamma)\vdash S(f):S(\mathit{U})\sqsubseteq_{S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
.

Proof.

(1)

By induction on the derivation of
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

Case (
P_TyVar
):
:

We are given
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{X}
for some
\mathit{X}
.
By definition,
S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X}))
.
By (
P_TyVar
),
S(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X}))\sqsubseteq_{S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{X}
.

Otherwise:
:

Obvious.

(2)

By induction on the derivation of
\langle\Gamma\vdash f:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
using Lemma
A.40
.1.
∎

Lemma A.41.

Suppose
\forall\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{U^{\prime}}).S(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})
and

\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{U^{\prime}})\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime})
.
If
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
, then
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

Proof.

By induction on the derivation of
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

Case (
P_IdBase
):
:

Obvious.

Case (
P_TyVar
):
:

We are given
S(\mathit{X})\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{X}
.
Since
S(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})
and
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime})
, we have

S(\mathit{X})\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}}\mathit{X}
by (
P_TyVar
).

Case (
P_Dyn
):
:

Obvious.

Case (
P_Arrow
):
:

By the IHs and (
P_Arrow
).
∎

Lemma A.42.

For any
S
and
\mathit{U}
such that
\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{U})\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S)
,

S(\mathit{U})\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\mathit{U}
.

Case
\mathit{U}=\iota
for some
\iota
:
:

Since
S(\mathit{U})=\iota
, we finish by (
P_IdBase
).

Case
\mathit{U}=\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
for some
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
:
:

We have
S(\mathit{U})=S(\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\!\rightarrow\!S(\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}})
.
By the IH,

S(\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and

S(\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
Thus, by (
P_Arrow
),

S(\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

Case
\mathit{U}=\mathit{X}
for some
\mathit{X}
:
:

By (
P_TyVar
).

Case
\mathit{U}=\star
:
:

By (
P_Dyn
).
∎

Lemma A.43.

Suppose that

\forall\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{2}}}).\textit{ftv}\,(S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})
.

(1)

If
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
,
then
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}})
.

(2)

If
\langle\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
,
then
\langle\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}}):S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{f^{\prime}})\dashv S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\Gamma^{\prime})\rangle
.

Proof.

(1)

By induction on the derivation of
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.
The only interesting case is (
P_TyVar
).
In that case, we are given
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{X}
and,
by inversion,
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}})
.
If
\mathit{X}\,\not\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{2}}})
, then
S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})=\mathit{X}
and it suffices to show that

S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{X}
, which is proved by (
P_TyVar
)
since
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})
.
Otherwise, if
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{2}}})
, then

\textit{ftv}\,(S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})
.
Thus, we have
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X}))\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})

by Lemma
A.42
.

(2)

By induction on the derivation of

\langle\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
with the first case.
∎

Lemma A.44.

Suppose
\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})
and

\forall\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}).\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.

(1)

If
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
,
then
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}(\mathit{U^{\prime}})
.

(2)

If
\langle\Gamma\vdash f:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
,
then
\langle\Gamma\vdash f:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}(\mathit{U^{\prime}}):S^{\prime}(f^{\prime})\dashv S^{\prime}(\Gamma^{\prime})\rangle
.

Proof.

(1)

By induction on the derivation of
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

Case (
P_TyVar
):
:

We are given
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{X}
for some
\mathit{X}
.
By inversion,
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.
So,
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})=S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})
.
Thus,
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}}\mathit{X}
by (
P_TyVar
).
By Lemma
A.43
,

S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})
.

Otherwise:
:

Obvious.

(2)

By induction on the derivation of
\langle\Gamma\vdash f:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle

∎

Lemma A.45.

If
\langle\emptyset\vdash w:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star:(w^{\prime}:\mathit{G^{\prime}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star)\dashv\emptyset\rangle

and
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{G^{\prime}}
,
then
\langle\emptyset\vdash w:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{G^{\prime}}:w^{\prime}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,

Proof.

By induction on the term precision derivation.
There are three interesting cases.

Case (
P_Cast
):
:

We are given
\langle\emptyset\vdash(w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}):\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star:(w^{\prime}:\mathit{G^{\prime}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star)\dashv\emptyset\rangle

and, by inversion,

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{G^{\prime}}:w^{\prime}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
and

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\mathit{U}
.
By (
P_CastL
), we finish.

Case (
P_CastL
):
:

We are given

\langle\emptyset\vdash(w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}):\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star:(w^{\prime}:\mathit{G^{\prime}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star)\dashv\emptyset\rangle

and, by inversion,

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star:(w^{\prime}:\mathit{G^{\prime}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star)\dashv\emptyset\rangle
and

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\mathit{U}
.
Since
w=w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}
is a value,
we have
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{G^{\prime}}
by Lemma
A.38
.
By the IH,
\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{G^{\prime}}:w^{\prime}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.
Since
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\mathit{U}
and
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{G^{\prime}}
,
we finish by (
P_CastL
).

Case (
P_CastR
):
:

By inversion.
∎

Lemma A.46.

If
\langle\emptyset\vdash(f:\mathit{U}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\star):\star\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

then
\langle\emptyset\vdash f:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,

Proof.

By induction on the term precision derivation.
There are three interesting cases.

Case (
P_Cast
):
:

We are given

\langle\emptyset\vdash(f:\mathit{U}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\star):\star\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:(f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}})\dashv\emptyset\rangle

and, by inversion,

\langle\emptyset\vdash f:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
and

\star\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
and

\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.
Since
\star\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
, we have
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\star
.
Thus,
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
, and by (
P_CastR
),

\langle\emptyset\vdash f:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:(f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}})\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.

Case (
P_CastL
):
:

By inversion.

Case (
P_CastR
):
:

Similar to the case of (
P_Cast
).
∎

Lemma A.47.

If
\langle\emptyset\vdash w:\iota\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\iota:w^{\prime}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,
then there exists
\mathit{c}
such that
w=w^{\prime}=c
.

Proof.

By case analysis on the term precision rule applied last.
∎

Lemma A.48 (Catch up to Value on the Left).

If
\langle\emptyset\vdash w:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,
then there exists
S^{\prime}
,
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}
, and
w^{\prime}

such that

•

f^{\prime}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,w^{\prime}
,

•

\langle\emptyset\vdash w:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}(\mathit{U^{\prime}}):w^{\prime}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,

•

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})
,

•

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\cap\textit{ftv}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X}))=\emptyset
, and

•

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}).\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.

Proof.

By induction on the term precision derivation.

Case (
P_Const
) and (
P_Abs
):
:

Obvious.

Case (
P_Cast
):
:

We are given,

\langle\emptyset\vdash(w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}):\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:(f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}})\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,
where
w=w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}
and
f^{\prime}=f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}}

for some
w_{{\mathrm{1}}}
,
f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
,
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
, and
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
,
\ell
, and
\ell^{\prime}
.
By inversion,

•:

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,

•:

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
, and

•:

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\mathit{U}
and
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

By the IH, there exist
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}
,
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
such that

•:

f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
,

•:

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}):w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,

•:

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})
,

•:

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\cap\textit{ftv}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X}))=\emptyset
, and

•:

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}).\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.

Thus, by (
E_Step
),

f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}}).

By case analysis on
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

Case
\iota\sim\iota
for some
\iota
:
:

Since
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
and
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\iota
,
we have
\mathit{U}=\iota
.
However, this contradicts the fact that
w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}

is a value.

Case
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\sim\mathit{X^{\prime}}
for some
\mathit{X^{\prime}}
:
:

By case analysis on
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
.

Case
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\iota
:
:

Similarly to the case
\iota\sim\iota
.

Case
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\mathit{X^{\prime\prime}}
for some
\mathit{X^{\prime\prime}}
:
:

By Lemma
A.35
,
\emptyset\vdash w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{X^{\prime\prime}}
.
This contradicts Lemma
2
.

Case
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
is a function type:
:

We show that

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}}):w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})\dashv\emptyset\rangle.

Since
\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{X^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,
we have
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{X^{\prime}}
by Lemma
A.34
.
Thus, since
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{X^{\prime}}
,
we have
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}=\mathit{U}
by Lemma
A.36
.
Since
\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}}):w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,
we have
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})

by Lemma
A.34
.
Thus,
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
.
Since
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
is a value (note that

S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
is a function type),

S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})\sim S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
, and
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
,
we finish by (
P_Cast
).

Case
\star\sim\star
:
:

By (
R_IdStar
),
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\star)\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\star)\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.
By definition,
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star
.
Thus, by (
P_CastL
), we have

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle,

which is what we want to show.

Case
\star\sim\mathit{U^{\prime}}
where
\mathit{U^{\prime}}\neq\star
:
:

Here,
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\star)=\star
.
By Lemma
2
,
there exist
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}
and
\mathit{G^{\prime}}
such that
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}=w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\mathit{G^{\prime}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star
.
By case analysis on
\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

Case
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\iota
for some
\iota
:
:

Here,
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\iota
, that is,
\mathit{U}=\iota
.
However, this contradicts the fact that

w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}
is a value.

Case
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
:
:

Here,
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\star\!\rightarrow\!\star)=\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
.
By Lemma
A.34
,
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
.
By definition,
\mathit{U}
is a function type.
Since
w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}
is a value,

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
is also a function type.
So, by definition,
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
.
By case analysis on
\mathit{G^{\prime}}
.

Case
\mathit{G^{\prime}}=\iota
for some
\iota
:
:

We are given,

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\iota\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.
This contradicts
Lemma
A.39
with
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
.

Case
\mathit{G^{\prime}}=\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
:
:

By (
R_Succeed
),
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}
.
By Lemma
A.45
,

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.
Since
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
, we have, by (
P_CastL
),

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star:w^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle,

which is what we want to show.

Case
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
for some
\mathit{X^{\prime}}
:
:

If
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
is not a type variable,
then we can prove as the other cases.

Here,
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
because
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
is generated by DTI.
By case analysis on
\mathit{G^{\prime}}
.

Case
\mathit{G^{\prime}}=\iota
for some
\iota
:
:

By Lemma
A.34
,

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{X^{\prime}}
.
By Lemma
2
,

\mathit{U}
is the dynamic type or a function type.
By case analysis on
\mathit{U}
.

Case
\mathit{U}
is the dynamic type:
:

Cannot happen since
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{X^{\prime}}
.

Case
\mathit{U}
is a function type:
:

By Lemma
2
,

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
is also a function type.
By definition, we have
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
but
this contradicts Lemma
A.39
with

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star:(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\mathit{G^{\prime}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star)\dashv\emptyset\rangle

(note that
\mathit{G^{\prime}}
is
\iota
).

Case
\mathit{G^{\prime}}=\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
:
:

We are given

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star:(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star)\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.
By Lemma
A.34
,

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{X^{\prime}}
.
By Lemma
2
,

\mathit{U}
is the dynamic type or a function type.
The case
\mathit{U}
is the dynamic type contradicts

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{X^{\prime}}
.
So,
\mathit{U}
is a function type and
there exist
\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
such that
\mathit{U}=\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}

such that
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}

since
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{X^{\prime}}
.
By Lemma
2
,
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
is also a function type.
By definition,
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
.

By (
R_InstArrow
) and (
R_Succeed
),

w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}):\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}

where
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}=[\mathit{X^{\prime}}:=\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}]
and
\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
are fresh.

Let
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}=[\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:=\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:=\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}]
.
It suffices to show that:

(1)

\displaystyle\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})(\mathit{X})

(2)

\displaystyle\langle\emptyset\vdash(w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}):\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}}):(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}):\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\dashv\emptyset\rangle

(3)

\displaystyle\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\cap\textit{ftv}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X}))=\emptyset

(
3
) is obvious.

For (
1
),
let
\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.
If
\mathit{X}=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
, then

\begin{array}[]{l@{ \quad= \quad}ll}(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})(\mathit{X^{\prime}})&(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})&\text{(since $S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\mathit{X^{\prime}}$)}\\
&(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\\
&S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})&\text{(since $\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}$ and $\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}$ are fresh)}\\
&\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\\
&S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})&\text{(since $S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}$)}\end{array}

Otherwise, suppose
\mathit{X}\neq\mathit{X^{\prime}}
.
By case analysis on whether
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\in\,\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X}))
or not.

Case
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\in\,\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X}))
:
:

Since
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\in\,\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X}))
,

\mathit{X}\neq\mathit{X^{\prime}}
, and
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
is generated by DTI,

\mathit{X^{\prime}}
is generated as a fresh type variable during evaluation of

f^{\prime}
.
Thus, we can suppose that
\mathit{X^{\prime}}
does not appear in
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}
.
However, it contradicts the fact that
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
,
that is,
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.

Case
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\not\in\,\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X}))
:
:

\begin{array}[]{l@{ \quad= \quad}ll}(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})(\mathit{X})&(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})&\text{(since $\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\not\in\,\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X}))$)}\\
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})&\text{(since $S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})$)}\\
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})&\text{(since $\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}$ and $\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}$ are fresh)}\end{array}

For (
2
),
by (
P_Cast
), it suffices to show that:

(4)

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star:S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}})\dashv\emptyset\rangle

(5)

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})

Since

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star:(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star)\dashv\emptyset\rangle
and

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
,
we have

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

by Lemma
A.45
.
Thus, by Lemma
A.44
,
(
4
) holds
if we have

(6)

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X}),

which we will show now.
Let
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.
If
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}})
, then, since
\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\cap\textit{ftv}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X}))=\emptyset
,
we have
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})
.
If
\mathit{X}=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
,

S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})(\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}})=\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}=S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})
.
Otherwise, if
\mathit{X}\neq\mathit{X^{\prime}}
,

S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})(\mathit{X})=S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})
.
If
\mathit{X}\,\not\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}})
, then
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})
.
Since
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}})
,

S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})
.
Thus, we have (
6
).

Finally, (
5
) is shown
by applying Lemma
A.44
to

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{X^{\prime}}
and (
1
).

Case
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
for some
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}
and
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
where
\mathit{U^{\prime}}\neq\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
:
:

By Lemma
A.34
,
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
.
By definition,
\mathit{U}
is a function type.
By Lemma
2
,
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
is a function type.
By definition,
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
.
By case analysis on
\mathit{G^{\prime}}
.

Case
\mathit{G^{\prime}}=\iota
for some
\iota
:
:

We are given,

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\iota\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.

A.39
.

Case
\mathit{G^{\prime}}=\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
:
:

We are given,

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.
By definition,

w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}})\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}})
.

A.45
,

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

,

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}})
.
We finish by (

).

Case
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\star
where
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\neq\star
:
:

By case analysis on
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
.

Case
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})
is a ground type:
:

Obvious.

Case

is not a ground type:

\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}
and
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
such that
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})=\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
.

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}):w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,
we have

by Lemma
A.34
.
By definition,
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
is a function type.
By Lemma
2
,

\mathit{U}
is the dynamic type or a function type.
By case analysis on
\mathit{U}
.

\mathit{U}=\star
:
:

2
,
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}=\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
.

\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})

and
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})
is not a ground type.
Contradiction.

\mathit{U}
is a function type:
:

Since
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\star
and
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})
is not a ground type,

w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star

by (
R_Ground
).
We finish by (
P_CastR
) and (
P_Cast
).

Case
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\sim\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{13}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{14}}}}
for some

Case (
P_CastL
):
:

Here,
\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

w_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and

By inversion,

•:

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,

•:

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
, and

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\mathit{U}
.

By the IH, there exist
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}
,
S^{\prime}
, and
w^{\prime}
such that

•:

f^{\prime}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,w^{\prime}
,

•:

,

•:

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})
,

•:

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\cap\textit{ftv}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X}))=\emptyset
, and

•:

.

Since
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
,

A.44
.

Case (

):

\langle\emptyset\vdash w:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

Thus, by (

),

f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}}).

A.39
.

Case
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
for some
