:
:

If
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
is not a type variable,
then we can prove as the other cases.

Here,
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
because
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
is generated by DTI.
By case analysis on
\mathit{G^{\prime}}
.

Case
\mathit{G^{\prime}}=\iota
for some
\iota
:
:

By case analysis on
\mathit{U}
.

Case
\mathit{U}=\iota
:
:

By definition,
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\iota
.
By Lemma
A.45
,

\langle\emptyset\vdash w:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\iota:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.

By (
R_InstBase
),
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\iota\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}})

where
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}=[\mathit{X^{\prime}}:=\iota]
.

We show

(7)

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})(\mathit{X}).

Let
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.
If
\mathit{X}=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
, then, since
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
is generated by DTI,
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
.
Thus,
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\iota
.
Since
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{X^{\prime}}
(note that
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
) and

\mathit{U}=\iota
,
we have
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\iota
.
Thus,
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
.
Otherwise, suppose that
\mathit{X}\neq\mathit{X^{\prime}}
.
By case analysis on whether
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\in\,\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X}))
or not.

Case
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\in\,\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X}))

Since
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\in\,\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X}))

\mathit{X}\neq\mathit{X^{\prime}}
, and
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
is generated by DTI,

\mathit{X^{\prime}}
is generated as a fresh type variable during evaluation of

f^{\prime}
.
Thus, we can suppose that
\mathit{X^{\prime}}
does not appear in
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}
.
However, it contradicts the fact that
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\iota
,
that is,
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.

Case
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\not\in\,\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X}))
:

We have
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})(\mathit{X})
.

By Lemma
A.44
with
(
7
), we have

\langle\emptyset\vdash w:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\iota:S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}})\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.

\mathit{U}=\mathit{G}
where
\mathit{G}\neq\iota
:
:

This contradicts Lemma
A.39
.

\mathit{U}
is a function type:

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
.
This contradicts Lemma
A.39
.

Case
\mathit{U}=\star
:
:

Cannot happen since
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

\mathit{U}=\mathit{X^{\prime\prime}}
for some
\mathit{X^{\prime\prime}}
:
:

Cannot happen by Lemma
2
.

Case
\mathit{G^{\prime}}=\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
:

By case analysis on
\mathit{U}
.

Case

where
\mathit{G}\neq\star\!\rightarrow\!\star

\mathit{U}=\star

Case
\mathit{U}
is a function type:

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
.
By Lemma
A.45
,

\langle\emptyset\vdash w:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.

By (
R_InstArrow
),
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}):\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}

where
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}=[\mathit{X^{\prime}}:=\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}]
and
\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
are fresh.
Since
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
and
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
, we have
there exist some
\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
such that

S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\mathit{U}=\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

Let
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}=[\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:=\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:=\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}]
.
It suffices to show that:

(8)

\displaystyle\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})(\mathit{X})

(9)

\displaystyle\langle\emptyset\vdash w:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})(\mathit{U^{\prime}}):(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\dashv\emptyset\rangle

(10)

\displaystyle\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\cap\textit{ftv}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X}))=\emptyset

(
10
) is obvious.

We first show that

(11)

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X}).

Let
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.
If
\mathit{X}=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
, then

S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})=\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T_{{\mathrm{2}}}}=S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
.
Since
\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\cap\textit{ftv}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}}))=\emptyset
by the IH,
we have
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
.
Otherwise, if
\mathit{X}\neq\mathit{X^{\prime}}
, then

S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})
.

We show (
8
).
Let
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.
We have

\begin{array}[]{l@{\quad= \quad}ll}(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})(\mathit{X})&S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})&\text{(by (\ref{req:prec_catch_up_left_value:five-one}))}\\
&S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})&\text{(by the IH)}\end{array}

We show (
9
).
Since
\langle\emptyset\vdash w:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,
we have
\langle\emptyset\vdash w:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star:S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}})\dashv\emptyset\rangle

by Lemma
A.44
with
(
11
).
Since
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
and
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
,
we have
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})(\mathit{X^{\prime}})

by Lemma
A.44
with
(
8
).
Since
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}})=w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}
and

(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})(\mathit{U^{\prime}})=(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
,
we have (
9
) by (
P_CastR
).

Case
\mathit{U}=\mathit{X^{\prime\prime}}
for some
\mathit{X^{\prime\prime}}
:
:

Cannot happen by Lemma
2
.

Case
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
for some
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}
and
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
where
\mathit{U^{\prime}}\neq\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
:
:

By Lemma
A.34
,
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
.
By definition,
\mathit{U}
is a function type.
So, by definition,
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
.
By Lemma
A.44
,

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}})
.

By (
R_Expand
),

w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}})\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}})
.
We finish by (
P_CastR
).

Case
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\star
where
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\neq\star
:
:

By case analysis on
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})
.

Case
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})
is a ground type:
:

Obvious.

Case
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})=\star
:
:

Cannot happen.

Case
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
for some
\mathit{X^{\prime}}
:
:

Since
\langle\emptyset\vdash w:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}):w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,
we have
\emptyset\vdash_{\textsf{{B}}}w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{X^{\prime}}
, which
contradicts Lemma
2
.

Case
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})=\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
for some
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}
and
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
:
:

.
By Lemma
A.34
,

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})
.
By definition,
\mathit{U}
is a function type.
So,
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star\!\rightarrow\!\star
.
By (
R_Ground
),

w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\star
.
We finish by (
P_CastR
).

Case
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\sim\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{13}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{14}}}}
for some
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}
,
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
,
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{13}}}}
, and
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{14}}}}
:
:

Obvious.

Otherwise:
:

Cannot happen. ∎

Lemma A.49.

If
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{T}
, then
\mathit{U}=S(\mathit{T})
.

Proof.

By induction on the derivation of
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{T}
.

Case (
P_IdBase
):
:

Obvious.

Case (
P_TyVar
):
:

Obvious.

Case (
P_Dyn
):
:

Cannot happen.

Case (
P_Arrow
):
:

By the IHs.
∎

Lemma A.50.

If
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}})
,
then
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

Proof.

By induction on the derivation of
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}})
.

Case (
P_IdBase
):
:

We are given
\iota\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\iota
for some
\iota
such that

\mathit{U}=\iota
and
S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}})=\iota
.
If
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\iota
, then we finish by (
P_IdBase
).
Otherwise,
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\mathit{X}
and
S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})=\iota
for some
\mathit{X}
.
Since
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})=\iota
, we have

\iota\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{X}
by (
P_TyVar
).

Case (
P_TyVar
):
:

We are given
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{X}
for some
\mathit{X}
such that

\mathit{U}=S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})
and
S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}})=\mathit{X}
.
By inversion, we have
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})
.
Since
S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}})=\mathit{X}
, there exist
\mathit{X^{\prime}}
such that

\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
and
S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\mathit{X}
.
That is, we have
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
.
We show that
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{X^{\prime}}
.
If
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{2}}})
, then we finish by (
P_TyVar
)
since
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})
.
Otherwise, suppose that
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\not\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{2}}})
.
Since
S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=\mathit{X}
,
\mathit{X}=\mathit{X^{\prime}}
.
Since
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})
, we have
\mathit{X^{\prime}}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{1}}})
.
Since
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})=S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
, we have

S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{X^{\prime}}
by (
P_TyVar
).

Case (
P_Dyn
):
:

By (
P_Dyn
) since
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\star
.

Case (
P_Arrow
):
:

We are given
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
for some

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
,
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
,
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
, and
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
such that

\mathit{U}=\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}})=\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
By inversion,
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
By case analysis on
\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

Case
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\mathit{X}
for some
\mathit{X}
:
:

Since
S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})=\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
, there exist
\mathit{T^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{T^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
such that

\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}=\mathit{T^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}=\mathit{T^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
We have
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{T^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
By Lemma
A.49
,

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}=S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{T^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{T^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})=S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X}))
.
Thus, by (
P_TyVar
),
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{X}
.

Case
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
for some
\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
:
:

We have
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}=S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})
and
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}=S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})
.
Since
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})
,
we have
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}

by the IHs.
By (
P_Arrow
), we have

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

Otherwise:
:

Contradiction.
∎

Lemma A.51.

If
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
and
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
,
then, for any
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{U^{\prime}})
,
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})=S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})
.

Proof.

By induction on the derivation of
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

Case (
P_IdBase
):
:

Obvious since
\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{U^{\prime}})=\emptyset
.

Case (
P_TyVar
):
:

We are given
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{X}
for some
\mathit{X}
such that

\mathit{U}=S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})
and
\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\mathit{X}
.
Since
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{X}
, we have

S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})=S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{X})
; note that the precision rules applicable
in the case that types on the right-hand side are type variables
are only (
P_TyVar
).

Case (
P_Dyn
):
:

Obvious since
\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{U^{\prime}})=\emptyset
.

Case (
P_Arrow
):
:

By the IHs.
∎

Lemma A.52.

If

•

\langle\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]\uplus S}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
and

•

\mathit{U}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U^{\prime}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]
and

•

for any
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S)
,
\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}\cap\textit{ftv}\,(S(\mathit{X}))=\emptyset
and

\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}\cap\textit{ftv}\,(S(\mathit{X}))=\emptyset
and

•

\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}\cap\textit{ftv}\,(\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}})=\emptyset
and

•

For any
\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{k}}}\,\in\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}\setminus\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{U^{\prime}})
,
\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{k}}}
is a fresh type
variable (we write
\mathit{P}
for the set of such type variables

\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{k}}}
),

then there exist
S^{\prime}
such that

•

\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime})=\mathit{P}
and

•

for any
\mathit{X}=\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{k}}}\in\mathit{P}
,
S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})=\mathit{T^{\prime\prime}_{\mathit{k}}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]
and

•

\langle\Gamma[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\vdash\mathit{f}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]:\mathit{U}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\sqsubseteq_{S\uplus S^{\prime}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]:\mathit{f^{\prime}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]\rangle
.

Proof.

Since
\langle\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]\uplus S}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
,
we have

\langle\Gamma[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\vdash\mathit{f}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]:\mathit{U}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\sqsubseteq_{[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\circ([\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]\uplus S)}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle

by Lemma
A.40
.
Since, for any
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S)
,
\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}\cap\textit{ftv}\,(S(\mathit{X}))=\emptyset
,
we have
[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\circ([\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]\uplus S)=[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]}]\uplus S
.
Thus,

\langle\Gamma[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\vdash\mathit{f}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]:\mathit{U}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\sqsubseteq_{[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]}]\uplus S}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle

Let
\overrightarrow{{\mathit{X}}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}
be
\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}\cap\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{U^{\prime}})
and

\overrightarrow{{\mathit{X}}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}
be
\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}\setminus\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{U^{\prime}})
.
Then,

(12)

\langle\Gamma[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\vdash\mathit{f}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]:\mathit{U}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\sqsubseteq_{[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime\prime}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]}]\uplus[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime\prime}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]}]\uplus S}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle

By Lemma
A.34
,

\mathit{U}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\sqsubseteq_{[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\circ([\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]\uplus S)}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.
Since
\mathit{U}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U^{\prime}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]
,
we have

\mathit{U}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\sqsubseteq_{S\circ[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]}\mathit{U^{\prime}}

by Lemma
A.50
.
Thus, by Lemma
A.51
,

\text{for any }\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{ftv}\,(\mathit{U^{\prime}}),[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\circ([\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]\uplus S)(\mathit{X})=S\circ[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}](\mathit{X})

Since
\textit{dom}\,(S)\cap\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}=\emptyset
,
for any
\mathit{k}
such that
\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{k}}}\,\in\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}
,

(13)

\mathit{T^{\prime\prime}_{\mathit{k}}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]=S(\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{k}}})

Here,

\begin{array}[]{llll}&[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime\prime}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]}]\uplus[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime\prime}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]}]\uplus S\\
=&[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{S({\mathit{T}}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime})}]\uplus[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime\prime}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]}]\uplus S&\text{(by (\ref{eq:type_app_preserve_term_prec:two}))}\\
=&[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime\prime}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]}]\uplus(S\circ[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}])\\
=&[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime\prime}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]}]\circ S\circ[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}]\\
&\lx@intercol\hfil\text{(since $\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}\cap\textit{ftv}\,(\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}})=\emptyset$ and for any $\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S)$ $\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}\cap\textit{ftv}\,(S(\mathit{X}))=\emptyset$)}\lx@intercol\end{array}

Thus, from (
12
),

\langle\Gamma[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\vdash\mathit{f}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]:\mathit{U}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\sqsubseteq_{[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime\prime}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]}]\circ S\circ[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}]}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle

By Lemma
A.43
,

\langle\Gamma[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\vdash\mathit{f}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]:\mathit{U}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\sqsubseteq_{[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime\prime}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]}]\uplus S}\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime\prime}\rangle

where
\mathit{U^{\prime\prime}}=\mathit{U^{\prime}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}]
and
\mathit{f^{\prime\prime}}=\mathit{f^{\prime}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}]
and
\Gamma^{\prime\prime}=\Gamma^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{1\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}]
.
Since
\overrightarrow{{\mathit{T}}_{2\mathit{j}}^{\prime}}
are type variables,

\langle\Gamma[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\vdash\mathit{f}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]:\mathit{U}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\sqsubseteq_{S\uplus S^{\prime}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]:\mathit{f^{\prime}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]\rangle

where
S^{\prime}
is the type substitution described in the statement.
∎

Lemma A.53.

Let type variables in
\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{2}}})
be fresh.

(1)

If
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
,
then
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\uplus S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

(2)

If
\langle\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
,
then
\langle\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\uplus S_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
.

Proof.

By induction on the derivations of

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
and
\langle\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
.xo
∎

Lemma A.54.

If

•

\langle\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}.\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime},\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}.\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rangle
and

•

\langle\Gamma\vdash w:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]\uplus S}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:w^{\prime}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
and

•

\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}
and
\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}
does not occur free in the derivation of

\langle\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}.\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime},\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}.\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rangle
,

then there exist
S^{\prime}
such that

•

\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime})
is a set of fresh type variables and

•

\langle\Gamma\vdash\mathit{f}[\mathit{x}:=\Lambda\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}.\,w]:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S\uplus S^{\prime}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}[\mathit{x}:=\Lambda\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}.\,w^{\prime}]\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle
.

Proof.

By induction on
\langle\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}.\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{f}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\Gamma^{\prime},\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}.\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rangle
.

Case (
P_VarP
):
:

We are given

\langle\Gamma,\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}.\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{x^{\prime}}[\overrightarrow{\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}_{\mathit{i}}}]:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:\mathit{x^{\prime}}[\overrightarrow{{\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}_{\mathit{j}}}^{\prime}}]\dashv\Gamma^{\prime},\mathit{x}:\forall\,\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}.\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rangle

for some
\mathit{x^{\prime}}
,
\overrightarrow{\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}_{\mathit{i}}}
and
\overrightarrow{{\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}_{\mathit{j}}}^{\prime}}
.
If
\mathit{x}\neq\mathit{x^{\prime}}
, then obvious.
Otherwise, we suppose that
\mathit{x}=\mathit{x^{\prime}}
.
Furthermore,
\nu
in
\overrightarrow{\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}_{\mathit{i}}}
and
\overrightarrow{{\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}_{\mathit{j}}}^{\prime}}
generate fresh
type variables.
We write
\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}
(resp.
\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}
) for types which are the same as

\overrightarrow{\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}_{\mathit{i}}}
(resp.
\overrightarrow{{\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}_{\mathit{j}}}^{\prime}}
) except that the occurrences of

\nu
are replaced with the fresh type variables.
Note that
\mathit{U}=\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}_{\mathit{i}}}]=\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]
and

\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{{\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}_{\mathit{j}}}^{\prime}}]=\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]
.
By inversion,

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\sqsubseteq_{S}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]
.
By Lemma
A.52
,
there exist
S^{\prime}
such that

\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime})
is the same as fresh type variables generated by

\nu
in
\overrightarrow{{\mathbb{\pdfliteral T\pdfliteral}_{\mathit{j}}}^{\prime}}
and

\langle\Gamma[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\vdash w[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\sqsubseteq_{S\uplus S^{\prime}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]:w^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]\rangle.

Since
\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}\cap\textit{ftv}\,(\Gamma)=\emptyset
and
\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}\cap\textit{ftv}\,(\Gamma^{\prime})=\emptyset
,
we have

\langle\Gamma\vdash w[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X_{\mathit{i}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T_{\mathit{i}}}}]\sqsubseteq_{S\uplus S^{\prime}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]:w^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\mathit{X^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}:=\overrightarrow{\mathit{T^{\prime}_{\mathit{j}}}}]\dashv\Gamma^{\prime}\rangle,

which is what we want to show.

Case (
P_Blame
):
:

Obvious by Lemmas
A.35
and

A.5
.

Otherwise:
:

By the IH(s) and Lemma
A.53
.
∎

Lemma A.55.

If
f_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,f_{{\mathrm{2}}}
where (
E_Abort
) is not applied,
then
\mathit{E}[f_{{\mathrm{1}}}]\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,S(\mathit{E})[f_{{\mathrm{2}}}]
.

Proof.

By induction on the number of steps of
f_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,f_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.
If the number of steps is zero, then
f_{{\mathrm{1}}}=f_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and
S=[]
.
Thus,
\mathit{E}[f_{{\mathrm{1}}}]=S(\mathit{E})[f_{{\mathrm{2}}}]
, and
\mathit{E}[f_{{\mathrm{1}}}]\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,\mathit{E}[f_{{\mathrm{1}}}]
.
If the number of steps is more than zero, there exist
\mathit{E^{\prime}}
,
f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
,

S_{{\mathrm{1}}}
,
S_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, and
f^{\prime}
such that

•

f_{{\mathrm{1}}}=\mathit{E^{\prime}}[f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}]
,

•

f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\xrightarrow{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,f^{\prime}
,

•

\mathit{E^{\prime}}[f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}]\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E^{\prime}}[f^{\prime}])
,

•

S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E^{\prime}}[f^{\prime}])\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,f_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, and

•

S=S_{{\mathrm{2}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.

By (
E_Step
),
\mathit{E}[\mathit{E^{\prime}}[f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}]]\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S_{{\mathrm{1}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{E^{\prime}}[f^{\prime}]])
.
By the IH,
S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E}[\mathit{E^{\prime}}[f^{\prime}]])\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,S_{{\mathrm{2}}}(S_{{\mathrm{1}}}(\mathit{E}))[f_{{\mathrm{2}}}]=S(\mathit{E})[f_{{\mathrm{2}}}]
.
Thus,
\mathit{E}[\mathit{E^{\prime}}[f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}]]\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,S(\mathit{E})[f_{{\mathrm{2}}}]
.
∎

Lemma A.56 (Simulation of Function Application).

If
\langle\emptyset\vdash\lambda\mathit{x}\!:\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\,\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

and
\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,
then there exists
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}
,
S^{\prime}
,
S^{\prime\prime}
, and
f^{\prime}
such that

•

w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{+}\hskip 1.99997pt\,f^{\prime}
where (
E_Abort
) is not applied,

•

\langle\emptyset\vdash\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\mathit{x}:=w_{{\mathrm{2}}}]:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}\uplus(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})}S^{\prime}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,

•

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})
,

•

type variables in
\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime})
are fresh, and

•

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}).\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.

Proof.

By induction on the number of casts that occur in
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.
By case analysis on the precision rule applied last to derive

\langle\emptyset\vdash\lambda\mathit{x}\!:\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\,\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.

Case (
P_Abs
):
:

We are given

\langle\emptyset\vdash\lambda\mathit{x}\!:\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\,\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\lambda\mathit{x}\!:\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\,\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

and
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}=\lambda\mathit{x}\!:\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\,\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
for some
\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
.
By inversion,

\langle\mathit{x}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\dashv\mathit{x}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\rangle

and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
.
By (
R_Beta
),
(\lambda\mathit{x}\!:\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\,\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\,w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\mathit{x}:=w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}]
.
By Lemma
A.54
,

\langle\emptyset\vdash\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\mathit{x}:=w_{{\mathrm{2}}}]:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\mathit{x}:=w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}]\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.
We finish by letting
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}
,
S^{\prime}
, and
S^{\prime\prime}
be empty.

Case (
P_CastR
):
:

By Lemma
2
,
there exist
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}
,
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}
,
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
, and
\ell^{\prime}
such that

\langle\emptyset\vdash\lambda\mathit{x}\!:\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\,\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

and
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}=w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

By inversion,

\langle\emptyset\vdash\lambda\mathit{x}\!:\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\,\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
By inversion of
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
, we have

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
By Lemma
A.34
,

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
.
By its inversion,

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
.

By (
R_AppCast
),

(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\,w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\,(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\bar{\ell^{\prime}}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}})):\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

By (
P_CastR
),

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\bar{\ell^{\prime}}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.

By Lemma
A.48
,
there exist
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}
,
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, and
w^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
such that

•:

w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\bar{\ell^{\prime}}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,w^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
,

•:

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}):w^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,

•:

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})
, and

•:

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}).\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.

So,

(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\,(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\bar{\ell^{\prime}}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}})):\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}})\,w^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}):S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}})\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})

by Lemma
A.55
.

By Lemma
A.44
,

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})
and

\langle\emptyset\vdash\lambda\mathit{x}\!:\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\,\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}):S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}})\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.

By the IH, there exist
S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}
,
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}
,
S^{\prime\prime}
, and
\mathit{f^{\prime\prime}}
such that

•:

S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}})\,w^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{+}\hskip 1.99997pt\,\mathit{f^{\prime\prime}}
where (
E_Abort
) is not applied,

•:

\langle\emptyset\vdash\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\mathit{x}:=w_{{\mathrm{2}}}]:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}\uplus(S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}):\mathit{f^{\prime\prime}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,

•:

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}(\mathit{X})
,

•:

type variables in
\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime})
are fresh, and

•:

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}).\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.

We have

(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}})\,w^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}):S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}})\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{+}\hskip 1.99997pt\,\mathit{f^{\prime\prime}}:S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}})\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})

by Lemma
A.55
.

By Lemma
A.44
,

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})
.
By Lemma
A.53
,

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}\uplus(S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})
.
Thus, by (
P_CastR
),

\langle\emptyset\vdash\mathit{f_{{\mathrm{1}}}}[\mathit{x}:=w_{{\mathrm{2}}}]:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}\uplus(S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):\mathit{f^{\prime\prime}}:S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}})\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\dashv\emptyset\rangle.

We show
\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})
.
Let
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.
We have
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})
.
Since
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
is generated by DTI,
free type variables in
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})
are fresh for
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}
.
Since
\forall\mathit{X^{\prime}},S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
,
it is found that
\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})
if
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})
.
If
\mathit{X}\,\not\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})
, then
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})=\mathit{X}
.
Thus,
\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})
, and so

S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})
.

Finally, it is obvious that
\forall\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}).\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.

Otherwise:
:

Cannot happen. ∎

Lemma A.57 (Simulation of Unwrapping).

If
\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

and
\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,
then there exists
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}
,
S^{\prime}
, and
f^{\prime}
such that

•

w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,f^{\prime}
where (
E_Abort
) is not applied,

•

\langle\emptyset\vdash(w_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,(w_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\bar{\ell}}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{11}}}})):\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):\mathit{f^{\prime}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,

•

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})
, and

•

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}).\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.

Proof.

By (
R_AppCast
),

(w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\,w_{{\mathrm{2}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,(w_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,(w_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\bar{\ell}}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{11}}}})):\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
By induction on the number of casts that occur in
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.
By case analysis on the precision rule applied last to derive

\langle\emptyset\vdash(w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.

Case (
P_Cast
):
:

Here, by Lemma
2
,
there exist
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}
,
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}
,
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
, and
\ell^{\prime}
such that

\langle\emptyset\vdash(w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

and
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}=w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

By inversion,

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}

A.34
,

By (
R_AppCast
),

(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\,w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\,(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\bar{\ell^{\prime}}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}})):\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

We finish by (
P_Cast
), (
P_App
), and (
P_Cast
).

Case (
P_CastL
):
:

Here,

\langle\emptyset\vdash(w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}

.

,

), (
P_App
), and (

).

):
:

Here, by Lemma
2
,
there exist

,
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}
,

\ell^{\prime}
such that

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

and
w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}=w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

By inversion,

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.
By inversion of
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
,

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

A.34
,

.

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}
and
\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}
.

By (
R_AppCast
),

(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\,w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\,(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\bar{\ell^{\prime}}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}})):\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

By (
P_CastR
),

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}:w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\bar{\ell^{\prime}}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.
By Lemma
A.48
,
there exist
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}
,
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, and
w^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
such that

•:

w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\bar{\ell^{\prime}}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,w^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
,

•:

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}):w^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,

•:

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})
, and

•:

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}).\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.

We have

(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\,(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\bar{\ell^{\prime}}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}})):\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}})\,w^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}):S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}})\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})

by Lemma
A.55
.
By Lemma
A.44
,

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})
and

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}):S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}})\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.

By the IH,
there exist
S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}
,
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}
, and
\mathit{f^{\prime\prime}}
such that

•:

S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}})\,w^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,\mathit{f^{\prime\prime}}
where (
E_Abort
) is not applied,

•:

\langle\emptyset\vdash(w_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,(w_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\bar{\ell}}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{11}}}})):\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}}):\mathit{f^{\prime\prime}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,

•:

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}(\mathit{X})
, and

•:

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}).\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.

We have

(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(w^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}})\,w^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}):S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}})\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,\mathit{f^{\prime\prime}}:S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}})\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})
by Lemma
A.55
.

By Lemma
A.44
,

\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})
.
By (
P_CastR
),

\langle\emptyset\vdash(w_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,(w_{{\mathrm{2}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\bar{\ell}}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{11}}}})):\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{12}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}}S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{32}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):\mathit{f^{\prime\prime}}:S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{32}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}})\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{32}}}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\dashv\emptyset\rangle.

where
S^{\prime\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}=S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}
and
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{32}}}=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

We show
\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})
.
Let
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.
We have
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})
.
Since
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
is generated by DTI,
free type variables in
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})
are fresh for
S_{{\mathrm{0}}}
.
Since
\forall\mathit{X^{\prime}},S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X^{\prime}})
,
it is found that
\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})
if
\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})
.
If
\mathit{X}\,\not\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})
, then
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})=\mathit{X}
.
Thus,
\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})
, and so

S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X})
.

Finally, it is obvious that
\forall\mathit{X}\,\in\,\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}).\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})
.
∎

Lemma A.58 (Simulation of More Precise Programs).

If
\langle\emptyset\vdash f_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
and
f_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,f_{{\mathrm{2}}}
,
then there exist
S^{\prime}
,
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}
,
S^{\prime\prime}
, and
f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
such that

•

f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
where (
E_Abort
) is not applied,

•

\langle\emptyset\vdash f_{{\mathrm{2}}}:S(\mathit{U})\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}\uplus(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})}S^{\prime}(\mathit{U^{\prime}}):f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,

•

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})
,

•

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}).\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})
, and

•

\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime})
is a set of fresh type variables.

Proof.

By induction on the term precision derivation.

Case (
P_Op
):
:

We are given
f_{{\mathrm{1}}}=\mathit{op}\,(f_{{\mathrm{11}}},f_{{\mathrm{12}}})
and
f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}=\mathit{op}\,(f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}},f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}})

for some
\mathit{op}
,
f_{{\mathrm{11}}}
,
f_{{\mathrm{12}}}
,
f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}
, and
f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}
.

By inversion,
there exist
\iota_{{\mathrm{1}}}
,
\iota_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, and
\iota
such that

•:

\mathit{ty}(\mathit{op})=\iota_{{\mathrm{1}}}\!\rightarrow\!\iota_{{\mathrm{2}}}\!\rightarrow\!\iota
,

•:

\langle\emptyset\vdash f_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\iota_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\iota_{{\mathrm{1}}}:f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
, and

•:

\langle\emptyset\vdash f_{{\mathrm{12}}}:\iota_{{\mathrm{2}}}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\iota_{{\mathrm{2}}}:f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{12}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

where
\mathit{U}=\mathit{U^{\prime}}=\iota
.

\langle\emptyset\vdash w_{{\mathrm{111}}}:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star:\star\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star:f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.
By (
P_CastL
),

\langle\emptyset\vdash(w_{{\mathrm{111}}}:\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star):\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star:f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.
By Lemma
A.40
,

\langle\emptyset\vdash(S(w_{{\mathrm{111}}}):\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star):\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\sqsubseteq_{S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star:f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.
Since
\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star
,
we have

\langle\emptyset\vdash(S(w_{{\mathrm{111}}}):\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\star\!\rightarrow\!\star\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\!\rightarrow\!\mathit{X_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\sqsubseteq_{S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\star:f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

by (
P_CastL
).

Otherwise:
:

Contradiction.

Case
\mathit{E}=\mathit{E_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\,\mathit{U}
for some

):
:

Here,
f_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\mathit{U_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell}\!\mathit{U}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}[]\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,\textsf{{blame}}\,\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}

.

).

Case (
P_CastR
):
:

We are given
f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}=f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}}

for some
f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}
,
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
, and
\ell^{\prime}
.
By inversion,

\langle\emptyset\vdash f_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,

\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
, and
\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\sim\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.
By the IH, there exist
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}
,
S^{\prime}
,
S^{\prime\prime}
,and
f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{21}}}

such that

•:

f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{21}}}
,

•:

\langle\emptyset\vdash f_{{\mathrm{2}}}:S(\mathit{U})\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}\uplus(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})}S^{\prime}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}):f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{21}}}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
,

•:

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}).S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}(\mathit{X})=S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S^{\prime}(\mathit{X})
,

•:

\forall\mathit{X}\in\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}).\textit{ftv}\,(S^{\prime}(\mathit{X}))\subseteq\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}})
, and

•:

\textit{dom}\,(S^{\prime\prime})
is a set of fresh type variables.

So,
f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{11}}}:\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!\mathit{U^{\prime}}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\,f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{21}}}:S^{\prime}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}(\mathit{U^{\prime}})
.

By Lemma
A.40
,

S(\mathit{U})\sqsubseteq_{S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.
By Lemma
A.44
,

.
By Lemma
A.53
,

S(\mathit{U})\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}\uplus(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})}S^{\prime}(\mathit{U^{\prime}})
.

By (
P_CastR
),

\langle\emptyset\vdash f_{{\mathrm{2}}}:S(\mathit{U})\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}\uplus(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})}S^{\prime}(\mathit{U^{\prime}}):f^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{21}}}:S^{\prime}(\mathit{U^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\Rightarrow^{\ell^{\prime}}\!S^{\prime}(\mathit{U^{\prime}})\dashv\emptyset\rangle
.

Otherwise:
:

Cannot happen. ∎

Theorem A.1 (Dynamic Gradual Guarantee of
\lambda^{\textsf{DTI}}_{\textsf{B}}
).

Suppose
\langle\emptyset\vdash f:\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}:f^{\prime}\dashv\emptyset\rangle
and
\emptyset\vdash f:\mathit{U}
.

(1)

\emptyset\vdash f^{\prime}:\mathit{U^{\prime}}
and
\mathit{U}\sqsubseteq_{S_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{U^{\prime}}
.

(2)

•

If
f\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,w
,
then

f^{\prime}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,w^{\prime}
and

\langle\emptyset\vdash w:S(\mathit{U})\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}\uplus(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})}S^{\prime}(\mathit{U^{\prime}}):w^{\prime}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

for some
w^{\prime}
,
S^{\prime}
,
S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}
, and
S^{\prime\prime}
.

•

If
f\!\Uparrow
, then
f^{\prime}\!\Uparrow
.

(3)

•

If
f^{\prime}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,w^{\prime}
,
then

f\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,w
and

\langle\emptyset\vdash w:S(\mathit{U})\sqsubseteq_{S^{\prime\prime}\uplus(S^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}\circ S\circ S_{{\mathrm{0}}})}S^{\prime}(\mathit{U^{\prime}}):w^{\prime}\dashv\emptyset\rangle

for some
w
,
S
,

, and

,
or

for some
\ell
and
S
.

•

If
f^{\prime}\,\xmapsto{\mathmakebox[0.4em]{}S^{\prime}\mathmakebox[0.3em]{}}\hskip-3.99994pt{}^{\ast}\hskip 1.99997pt\,\textsf{{blame}}\,\ell^{\prime}
,

,

and
S
.

and

.

(2)

with

and

.

•

Here, there is an infinite evaluation sequence from
f
,
and it contains infinite times application of (

).
By Lemma
A.56
,
there is an infinite evaluation sequence from

.

(3)

•

\mathit{f}
cannot diverge as it contradicts the second item of (2).

\mathit{f}
cannot diverge or evaluate to a value as it contradicts

\mathit{f}
